# 🗺️ DESIGN_COVERAGE — матриця «дизайн ↔ модуль ↔ код»

> 🟢 **КОРЕКЦІЯ S6 (2026-06-25):** таблиця нижче згенерована **2026-06-22, ДО S6-фронту** і місцями
> застаріла. Після S6 РЕАЛЬНО збудовано (рядки 06/07/08/15/18, що позначені MISS/PLHD/«не існує», —
> вже IMPL): **Інбокс** master-detail в обох апках (`routes/inbox/InboxPage.tsx`) · **notification read-API**
> (`routes/notifications/` GET/PATCH/read-all) · **матриця каналів** (`/profile/telegram/connect`-сусід:
> `/profile/notifications` GET+PATCH, UI `NotificationsSection`) · **Telegram-лінк** (API `routes/telegram/`
>
> - бот `apps/bot` `/start`→link) · **email** stone+lime палітра + 3 нові шаблони (status/comment/invoice) ·
>   **Документи-таб** (генерація+PDF+«Надіслати»). Деталі — TRACKER §SPRINT 6 + `docs/modules/{06,07,08}.md`.
>   Решта таблиці — для не-S6 модулів — лишається актуальною.

> **Єдине джерело істини про РЕАЛЬНИЙ стан коду по кожному екрану.** Згенеровано наскрізним аудитом
> `design-coverage-audit` (60 агентів, adversarial-verified) · **2026-06-22**.
>
> ⚠️ **Важливо про статуси:** у `DESIGN_SPEC_FULL.md` і `docs/modules/*` позначки **`✅` / `РЕЮЗ`** означають
> **«дизайн/специфікація готові»**, а НЕ «в продакшені». Аудит показав, що багато таких позначок читаються
> як «зроблено», хоча коду немає. **Реальний стан коду — лише в цій таблиці** (колонка `Код`).

## Як читати

- **ДизСтатус** — як каже ТЗ: `РЕЮЗ` (мокап 1:1) · `РОЗШИР` (домалювати) · `НОВЕ` (нема) · `unknown`.
- **Бекенд** — чи є API під дані екрана: `yes` / `partial` / `no`.
- **Код** — РЕАЛЬНИЙ стан: 🟢 `IMPL` (жива сторінка) · 🟡 `PLHD` (заглушка-маршрут) · ⚪ `MISS` (нема).
- **Відповідність** — для реалізованих: `✅` тримається дизайну · `⚠️ RISK` відхилення (токени/верстка/стани) · `—` н/д.

## Зведення

- **Екранів усього:** 567 (по 29 модулях)
- **Код:** 🟢 85 реалізовано · 🟡 30 заглушка · ⚪ 452 нема
- **Відповідність реалізованих:** ✅ 38 ок · ⚠️ 47 RISK (виглядає/поводиться не так)
- **Drift (розбіжності доки↔реальність):** 180 підтверджено adversarial-перевіркою (14 відкинуто як хибні)

## Матриця по модулях

### 01 — Auth

Auth's core happy-path is real and well-conformed; everything beyond it claimed by the docs is unbuilt. CODE: portal /login /register /forgot-password /reset-password /invite/:token are all IMPLEMENTED, and workspace has /login /forgot-password /reset-password /invite/:token. All use @workflo/ui AuthShell with --wf-\* tokens (no hardcoded hex → conformance OK). Backend exists for login/register/refresh/logout/me/forgot/reset/switch-agency plus invite get/accept and member/executor invite creation. DESIGN: portal-auth.jsx fully covers PortalLogin (email/phone/magic/2fa methods), Register steps 1-2, forgot, reset, invite, OtpInput, MethodSwitch, WizardSteps; onboarding.jsx covers the 5-step agency onboarding; workspace-p2b.jsx holds the workspace/security mockups (AuthMagicLink, AuthLinkSent, AuthMustChange, AuthEmailChange); workspace-minors.jsx has OAuthButtons. DRIFT: The big gap is that DESIGN_SPEC_FULL's Part-III table and modules/01-auth.md "Аудит-фіналізація" both present 2FA (TOTP), email-verification, active-sessions, Google OAuth, magic-link, email-change, mustChangePassword, company-switcher and telegram-OTP as decided/spec'd-with-✅, but NONE of these have any backend route or UI — grep across apps/api + apps/portal + apps/workspace returns zero hits. Implemented РОЗШИР login screens are stripped to base email+password (no MethodSwitch / Caps-Lock hint / remaining-attempts counter that the design draws). The spec's design-file footer still points at the legacy design-v2/project/ folder rather than design-v2/project/, and only lists portal-auth/onboarding/portal-settings/email-templates — omitting workspace-p2b.jsx and workspace-minors.jsx where the workspace-auth and OAuth mockups actually live (it even says "Відсутній (потрібно створити): workspace-auth.jsx").

### 02 — Orders

Module 02 has a solid implemented core that uses --wf-\* tokens cleanly (no hardcoded hex in any order page): Portal orders list + detail(chat/files), Workspace cross-order registry (board+table), order detail (chat/files/time tabs + status transitions), and the kanban dashboard on Home. Backend is genuinely strong — 9→4 status machine, transitions with reason, approval submit/decide, time logs, comments+SSE, and schema models for OrderStage/EstimateLine/requiresApproval/approvalStatus/advanceGatePct all exist. The biggest real gaps are: (1) Portal /orders/new is a dead Placeholder despite design РЕЮЗ + a ready POST /orders backend — a high-severity drift; (2) the 02-А approval gate has UI shell only — the portal banner has no approve/reject buttons and the workspace requiresApproval toggle is unbuilt, even though decideOrderApproval/submitOrderApproval are fully implemented (high-severity conformance gap); (3) the workspace status-change UI never sends the reason that on_hold/cancelled require, so those reasons always persist null. Documentation drift is concentrated in modules/02-orders.md's «Аудит-фіналізація» B/C/D/E sections, which mark Tags/SLA/Dependencies/Templates with ✅ although OrderTag/SlaPolicy/OrderDependency/OrderTemplate models do not exist in schema.prisma at all (DESIGN_SPEC_FULL correctly labels these НОВЕ, so the spec and the module doc disagree). DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.13.2's 🟢 verdicts are accurate as design+backend readiness but should not be read as 'screen shipped'. Triage, kanban list/timeline variants, estimate editor, advance-gate, tags/SLA/dependencies/templates UI, soft-delete restore, alt/compact detail, inbox, and all mobile shells remain unbuilt (consistent with their НОВЕ/РОЗШИР status).

### 03 — Chat & Comments

Module 03 has a strong real-time core but a thin UI relative to the spec. Backend: GET/POST/POST-read comments + SSE stream (commentsStream.ts, single chatBus, leak-guard, heartbeat) are genuinely implemented with solid tenant/IDOR guards; OrderChatRead read-tracking exists. Frontend: both apps implement a basic ChatTab at /orders/:id (portal public-only, workspace with internal-note stripe + 🔒internal send-checkbox), token-clean (--wf-\*). Everything richer is unbuilt: media bubbles, lightbox/PDF, reply-to, pin, in-chat search, typing, @mention picker, reactions, read-receipt avatars, 'new messages ↓', reconnect banner, drag&drop, bubble context-menu, edit/delete — all correctly marked НОВЕ in Part III and matching the bare backend (no PATCH/DELETE/reactions/replyTo/participants routes). Both inbox screens are unrouted (workspace) or Placeholder (portal). The notable doc drift: (1) the module doc's 'Аудит-фіналізація' marks edit/delete/reactions/reply ✅ while the same doc's API-status line and the actual routes show them as backlog/absent; (2) the РЕЮЗ 'file attachment in bubble' is not actually rendered or backed (no fileIds in schema); (3) Part III mockup links still point at the superseded design/ bundle and never cite the canonical design-v2/order-chat.jsx that the module doc itself names as delivered; (4) the @mention participant-picker is documented as 'канонічно' but its GET /participants route does not exist.

### 04 — Files & Storage

Backend core is genuinely strong and matches docs: all 5 file endpoints are IMPLEMENTED in apps/api/src/routes/files/index.ts (order-scoped upload/list, id-based serve with attachment-disposition + nosniff + magic-byte guard, soft delete), the SVG-stripped MIME allowlist lives in packages/types/src/files.ts, and storage keys are tenant-prefixed. The РОЗШИР base UI (UploadZone + FileRow + "Файли" tab) is IMPLEMENTED identically in both apps (apps/{portal,workspace}/src/routes/orders/FilesTab.tsx) and its 100MB hint is correctly aligned. Everything tagged НОВЕ in DESIGN_SPEC_FULL — chat attachments, image thumbnails, lightbox, inline PDF viewer, audio/video players, big-media upload progress, and the antivirus (scanStatus) badge — is MISSING from code: the chat tab (ChatTab.tsx) is text-only with no attachment rendering at all, and the OrderFile schema has none of scanStatus/thumbStoredAs/commentId/documentId/context. Those НОВЕ screens DO exist in design-v2 (richly, in order-chat.jsx: OrderLightbox w/ pdf.js, AV-scan states, thumbnails, video/audio, OrderFilesV2) — but DESIGN_SPEC_FULL's mockup column never points at order-chat.jsx for those rows (shows "—"), a mapping gap. The two biggest doc-drift items are modules/04-files.md marking Audit-фіналізація B (thumbnails/sharp) and C (S3 adapter + signed-URL serving) as "✅" when sharp/thumbnails don't exist, the S3/Hetzner adapter is a reject-everything Phase-2 stub, and there is no signed-URL serving (API streams the blob).

### 05 — Billing & Payments

Module 05 (Billing) is the most-built module in code but materially behind both the design spec and its own module doc. What actually ships: Portal /billing (charges+payments tabs, sticky requisites card, charge-approval modal — РОЗШИР, tokenized, backend-complete) and Workspace /billing as a single owner-only page with internal charges/payments/debtors tabs, plus separate top-level routes /payouts, /services (catalog CRUD), /admin-wallet, /finance, /margin, /projects — all backed by real APIs (createPayment, overview, charges, portalSummary, projects, payout, catalog, pnl, margin, chargeDiscount). Token conformance is clean (0 hardcoded hex in the implemented pages). The drift is structural and feature-level, not visual: (1) the design's single Workspace /billing hub with 6 BillingSubtabs (invoices/payments/debtors/payouts/services/wallet) is NOT how code is organized — no /billing/\* sub-routes exist, several subtabs became unrelated top-level routes, and the spec's route mappings (/billing/invoices, /billing/debtors, /billing/payouts, /billing/services, /billing/refunds, /billing/wallet) are fictional; (2) the documented MVP manual-payment-confirm modal has a complete idempotent backend but NO frontend UI (high-severity gap vs 05-billing.md §6); (3) Settings/Billing is missing the entire exchange-rate sub-screen despite specced endpoints + cron; (4) the whole 05-ПРОЕКТИ/owner-extension wave (refund/credit-note, write-off, manual-discount modal, dunning UI+policy editor, overdue badges, VAT fields, payment-terms cascade, providers, pay-button/public-invoice, client-360 finance tab, project detail screen) is largely MISSING in code — these are honestly marked НОВЕ/РЕЮЗ in DESIGN_SPEC but the РЕЮЗ status on refund/credit-note rows is easy to misread as code-done when it only means design-reuse. DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.1/§5.13 ✅ marks are design-readiness, not code-status, and are internally consistent.

### 06 — Documents (PDF)

Module 06 is design-complete but code-absent. The UA PDF kit (documents-screens.jsx: InvoiceDoc/CompletionActDoc/ReconciliationActDoc/SpecificationDoc/ContractDoc/DocumentsIndex/FakeQR) and the EU kit + public invoice page (workspace-documents-eu.jsx: EuInvoiceDoc/EuServiceAgreementDoc/EuServiceActDoc/EuStatementDoc/EuSowDoc/PublicInvoicePage/DocKitViewer) all exist in design-v2. In CODE, NOTHING of this module's product surface is implemented: Portal /documents is a generic Placeholder.tsx; Workspace has no /documents, no /admin/templates, and ClientDetailPage/ProjectsPage have no 360° Documents tab. The PDF engine packages/templates/src/index.ts generatePdf() is an explicit stub that THROWS 'not implemented yet. Planned for Sprint 6'. There are NO document API routes (no list/generate/pdf/sign) — apps/api/src/routes has no documents/ dir. The ONLY backend that exists is the client legal-requisites route (apps/api/src/routes/company/requisites.ts: GET/PATCH /portal/company/requisites + GET /company/clients/:id/requisites) which feeds the Юр-онбординг 'to'-party data — but it has no UI consuming it. Both modules/06-documents.md and DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.5/§5.13 honestly flag the backend block + stub; the main drift is mislinked design paths (docs point at the old design/ bundle, not design-v2/), one false Portal-mobile /documents='done' claim in §5.3, and the DESIGN_SPEC 'Релевантні файли' list pointing at design-v2/project/ instead of design-v2/project/.

### 07 — Notifications

Module 07 (Notifications) is, in code, almost entirely the notify() WRITE-path plus a dead bell icon — nothing of the consumer-facing notification module is built. Design is strong and broadly accurate: every mockup cited (inbox-screens.jsx GlobalInbox/InboxDetail/cards, portal-states.jsx BellDropdown/CmdKOverlay, workspace-notify.jsx's 10 screens incl. NfMatrix/NfDigest/NfThresholds/NfAnnounce/NfTest, round4-dlq.jsx R4Dlq, workspace-admin-settings.jsx AdminTemplates) exists with the claimed component names. Backend has only: write dispatch (services/notifications.ts → tx.notification.create) and the matrix WRITE endpoint PATCH /profile/notifications (updateNotifications.ts). There is NO read-feed/unread-count/mark-read API, so even the РЕЮЗ-design Inbox cannot be wired. In the apps: portal /inbox = Placeholder, workspace has no /inbox; the Topbar bell is a binary dot wired to a no-op onBell in both AppLayouts (no numeric badge, no SSE); portal/workspace settings have NO notifications matrix and (in portal) not even the 'Нотифікації' nav tab the spec claims exists; no /admin/notifications/\* routes for announcements/digest/escalation/templates/DLQ. The most serious doc drift is the module doc's section B marking the in-app center API as ✅ done — the same repo's DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.13.2 (line 546) correctly states 'read-feed/mark-read API нема'. Secondary drift: the spec's claim that a portal notifications nav-tab 'is' present (it is only in the design prototype), and several 'РЕЮЗ' design statuses that read as 'built' when no code exists. The §5.13 verdict (🟡 частково: model+write yes, read-feed no, announcements greenfield) is the most accurate single summary in the docs.

### 08 — Email Templates

Module 08 is a "channel, not a screen" module: its deliverables are (a) a set of transactional HTML email templates rendered outside the apps and (b) a tenant email-settings admin screen. DESIGN is the most complete layer: all 15 email mockups exist in design-v2/project/email-templates.jsx (EmailInvite…EmailDigest), plus AdminSmtp in workspace-admin-settings.jsx and the refund email in round4-billing.jsx. CODE is far behind the spec's РЕЮЗ ("done 1:1") claims: only 4 templates are actually implemented (renderWelcomeEmail, renderPasswordResetEmail, renderInviteExecutorEmail, renderInviteCompanyMemberEmail in packages/notifications/src/email/templates/), wired through dispatch.ts::renderEmailForEvent for exactly 3 events (auth.welcome, auth.password_reset, system.invite_sent). The other 11 designed templates (order_received, invoice, payment_ok, deadline, verify, pwd_changed, new_login, otp, email_change, mention, doc_ready, digest) are NOT implemented. The 4 that exist diverge materially from the mockups: render.ts hardcodes a BLUE palette (#0F62FE primary, #1F2933 text) instead of the lime/stone --wf-\* tokens the DESIGN_SPEC mandates, and they are plain renderLayout bodies, not the rich hero/panel/CodeBlock layouts shown in the mockups. The tenant email-settings screen (AdminSmtp + 3-level sender / DNS-verification / category→sender matrix) has NO code at all — there is no /admin/smtp route in apps/workspace and no SMTP/sender API in apps/api. Backend for the channel itself exists (mailer.ts, dispatch, NotificationLog) but the email-specific persistence the docs claim — messageId on NotificationLog, SuppressedEmail, AgencyEmailDomain models — does not exist in schema.prisma. DESIGN_SPEC marks every template screen as РЕЮЗ ("done 1:1"), which is the central drift: РЕЮЗ describes design reuse of MailClient/MailHead primitives, but a reader treating it as code-status would be wrong for 11/15 templates.

### 09 — Referral

Module 09 is partially built. Portal `/referrals` is IMPLEMENTED (apps/portal/src/routes/referrals/ReferralsPage.tsx) backed by a real `GET /portal/referral` route, and Workspace referral-program admin is IMPLEMENTED inside `/settings` (SettingsPage → ReferralForm, owner-only) backed by `GET/PATCH /admin/referral/settings`. However the implemented Portal page is a slim subset of the `portal-referrals.jsx` mockup: it has the code+link copy, a 2-Stat row, the 3-step explainer, and a referred-companies list, but OMITS the link-funnel (clicks→signups→payments, 09-Б), the 4-Stat funnel row, QR code + share buttons (Telegram/Email/LinkedIn/WhatsApp), the email-masked referral table (it renders company name + bonus instead), the next-tier progress bar, the give-get "both benefits" block (09-А), the bonus-expiry card (09-Г), and the rules/fair-play transparency blocks — plus the "Запросити email" modal and empty-funnel state. Several DESIGN_SPEC_FULL screens marked НОВЕ/РОЗШИР are genuinely absent in code (link funnel, expiry card, who-referred selector on /clients/:id, analytics list of all referral pairs). Docs drift: modules/09-referral.md lists API paths (`/company/referral`, `/bonus-balance`, `/workspace/settings/referral`, `/workspace/referrals`) that do NOT match the real routes; DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §5.1 marks `/referrals` as fully done (✅) with no note that the coded page drops most mockup content. Code itself is token-clean (all `--wf-*`, `formatMoney`, no hardcoded hex/`$`), so conformance risk is about omitted states/content, not styling.

### 10 — Loyalty

Module 10's core client surface is in good shape: Portal /loyalty (apps/portal/src/routes/loyalty/LoyaltyPage.tsx) is IMPLEMENTED to the 4-tier canon (NEW/REGULAR/PARTNER/VIP @ 0/1k/5k/15k @ 0/3/7/12%), uses --wf-\* tokens throughout (no hardcoded hex), and covers loading/error/progress/at-max-VIP states. Backend is robust — GET /loyalty/tiers (public), GET /workspace/companies/:id/loyalty (progress+history), POST .../override-discount (owner-only, audit-logged), tierOverride + LoyaltyTierHistory persisted, and a C16 recalc cron — actually ahead of UI in places. The drift is concentrated in three areas. (1) Stale design vs canon: the design mockup portal-loyalty.jsx is still the OLD 5-tier cashback/bonus version (DESIGN_SPEC_FULL note #1 already flags it 'КРИТИЧНА'), and DESIGN_SYSTEM.md:253 still describes /loyalty as '5-tier ladder + історія бонусів + spending chart' — none of which the shipped page has (correctly, since cashback belongs to modules 09/25). (2) Workspace settings (10-А/В/Г) are entirely unbuilt: no route, no per-agency tier config (tiers are hardcoded constants), though a design mockup now exists — but the spec mis-links it to workspace-wallet.jsx ReferralTiers when it actually lives in workspace-notify.jsx WsLoyaltySettings, and the '—' (no-mockup) status for 10-В/10-Г is stale. (3) The loyalty.tier_upgraded notification is documented as shipping but is explicitly deferred — event key exists, but no template in packages/notifications and the cron sends nothing. Minor: documented API paths omit the /workspace prefix, and the 'client-owner only / plan-gated' nav state is unimplemented (portal nav is unfiltered).

### 11 — Content & Blog

Module 11 (Content & Blog) is fully designed but essentially zero-implemented in code. DESIGN: all РЕЮЗ/РОЗШИР screens have mockups (workspace-content.jsx for blog/cases CMS, landing-blog.jsx for public blog, landing-cases.jsx for public cases, terminal-variant.jsx for home teasers), and two screens the DESIGN_SPEC Part III still lists as '—' (structured case editor 11-А + AI-draft-from-order) are in fact DELIVERED in workspace-case-editor.jsx. CODE: nothing ships — there is no /content route in apps/workspace/src/App.tsx, the landing app (apps/landing/src/app/page.tsx) is a static 'Landing skeleton is ready.' placeholder with no /blog, /blog/:slug, /cases or /cases/:slug routes, and no blog/content/case route is registered in apps/api/src/app.ts. BACKEND: only the BlogPost model + BlogPostType enum exist in schema.prisma (L776-799, L1112), but they are referenced nowhere in apps/ — and the schema is missing every planned addition (agencyId, SEO columns, aiModel/aiPromptVersion) plus the AgencyAiUsage quota model, so AI-generation (Аудит B) and SEO-tools (Аудит C) are scope-accepted but unbuilt. DOCS are largely accurate and self-aware (module doc L11/L47 flags the greenfield backend and missing columns; DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.13.2 correctly marks case-editor 🔴 greenfield); the main drift risks are the module-doc 'REWRITE-done' header and the B/C ✅ checkmarks reading as 'implemented', a stale '—' mockup link in DESIGN_SPEC Part III for the now-delivered case editor, the CasesSection→TerminalCases naming mismatch, and a minor excerptUk nullability error in the doc's model block.

### 12 — Team & Executors

Module 12 is overwhelmingly designed (24 screens, mostly mockup-backed across workspace-admin.jsx, workspace-reports.jsx, workspace-tasks.jsx, workspace-p2b.jsx) and reasonably backed by API (apps/api/src/routes/team/team.ts, team/payouts.ts, orders/timeLogs.ts, services/payout.ts with period-locking), but the implemented frontend is a thin slice: only the Payouts page (/payouts) and Invite-Accept (/invite/:token) are genuinely IMPLEMENTED and design-conformant (token-based, no hardcoded hex). The two Team roster pages (workspace /team and portal /team) and the executor Profile page are PLACEHOLDER EmptyStates ('скоро · S5'); everything else in the module — roster, edit-member, compensation-model form, capacity/utilization, timesheet-approval, KPI cards, reports (executors/departments/timesheet), floating timer, departments CRUD, permissions matrix, referral 'who-referred' field, workload widget — is MISSING from code with no routes (no reports/ dir exists in apps/workspace/src/routes). Main doc drift: docs/modules/12-team-executors.md is stale (Apr 12, marks payout/timesheet/KPI '✅' as if shipped) and points readiness at a non-existent TRACKER.md; DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.13 over-claims portal /team, workspace /settings/team and /settings/permissions as ✅ when they are placeholders or absent. Mockups still carry the un-reconciled 'viewer'/'superadmin' role drift the spec itself flagged, and the compensation-model radio the spec requires is neither designed nor coded.

### 13 — Settings

Module 13 (Settings) has a comprehensive design surface in design-v2 (portal-team.jsx, portal-settings.jsx, avatar-icons.jsx covering profile, appearance/density, company requisites, members, notifications matrix, security/telegram/sessions), but the implemented code covers only a thin slice and the DESIGN*SPEC_FULL status column overstates readiness. Coded reality: Portal exposes one flat /settings route (apps/portal/src/routes/settings/SettingsPage.tsx) rendering name-edit, theme+language chips, and password-change — all token-conformant but missing avatar, density, timezone, company, members, notifications, telegram, and sessions. Portal /team is an invite-input + EmptyState placeholder, not the РЕЮЗ members screen the spec claims. There is no /settings/* sub-routing or SettingsNav in code at all. Workspace /settings is a different screen entirely (payment requisites + referral program), and /profile is read-only; the module-13 Workspace mirror is unbuilt (spec correctly flags this as НОВЕ). Backend offers PATCH /profile, PATCH /profile/password, PATCH /profile/notifications, and POST /company/members/invite only — there is NO GET for notifications (so the matrix can't hydrate), and no avatar-upload, telegram, GET-members, company GET/PATCH, system/exchange-rate, refresh-rate, activity-log, or data-export endpoints despite modules/13-settings.md documenting all of them under a /settings/\_ prefix that doesn't match the real unprefixed routes. The highest-value drift: several screens marked РЕЮЗ (members, security/telegram/sessions, notifications channels) are actually MISSING or PLACEHOLDER, and the module doc's endpoint summary is largely aspirational versus the shipped API.

### 14 — Landing

Design is essentially complete; code is a bare skeleton; one doc is badly stale. The Landing app (apps/landing) contains only three source files — page.tsx ("Landing skeleton is ready."), layout.tsx (hardcoded lang="uk", no i18n provider), and api/health/route.ts. There is NO [locale] segment, NO middleware.ts, NO next-intl dependency, NO sitemap.ts/robots.ts, and NO landing.json translations. Therefore every screen in the DESIGN_SPEC_FULL Module-14 table is MISSING in code (the only live route, /api/health, is not a designed Module-14 screen). All referenced design-v2 mockups exist and were verified by export (EcoNav, TermPageShell, BootSequence, TerminalLanding/Hero/Services/Cases/About/Partners/Scale/Nav/404, BlogIndex/BlogArticle, ServicesIndex/ServicePage/AboutPage/ContactPage/Error500, CaseIndex/CasePage, PricingPage/LegalPage, BookingPage, CompanyPage/ProjectPage). The four screens DESIGN_SPEC marks НОВЕ (Problems, Testimonials, CTA-booking band, Cookie-consent) genuinely have no dedicated mockup file, consistent with the spec. Backend: there is NO contact, blog, pricing/plans, booking, cases, or testimonials API; a ContactForm Prisma model exists (schema.prisma:801) but is unwired and lacks the audit-required fields (ipAddress/status/captchaScore/agencyId), and no LandingContent/ConsentLog models exist. DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.1 (row ~175, "~5%, секцій немає") and §5.2 (all code cells ❌, "page.tsx — заглушка") accurately describe reality. The single materially stale doc is modules/14-landing.md, which declares "Статус: MVP" and documents middleware.ts, [locale] routing, sitemap.ts, robots.ts and next-intl as if implemented — none of which exists.

### 15 — Telegram Bot

Module 15 splits into two surfaces, and reality diverges sharply from the docs on both. (1) The OUTBOUND notification path is genuinely implemented: packages/notifications has Telegram render templates (templates/index.ts) and a sendTelegram adapter, and apps/api/src/services/notifications.ts dispatches by reading NotificationSettings.telegramChatId. (2) Everything that lets a human reach that path is MISSING. apps/bot/src/index.ts is a 14-line skeleton whose only handler is /start replying 'Workflo bot skeleton is online.' — no OTP account-linking, no /status//help//unlink, no /orders//timer//balance, no inline-button/callback or force-reply handlers, no commands/ or middleware/ dirs, and no /bot/webhook route in the API. There is no broadcast/bot-admin API and no /admin/bot workspace route, so all five round4-bot.jsx admin screens are design-only despite РЕЮЗ/РОЗШИР status. The portal SettingsPage.tsx has no Telegram bind section at all (Безпека is password-only) and no notification matrix, despite portal-settings.jsx:174-193 existing and DESIGN_SPEC marking the bind РЕЮЗ. Net effect: telegramChatId can never be populated, so the working push code is unreachable end-to-end. The most serious doc drift is 15-bot.md §B/§C/§D marking interactive commands, inline-buttons, and executor notifications as ✅ done — all three are false — plus stale TelegramAdapter/Profile code samples that contradict the function-based adapter and NotificationSettings source-of-truth the same doc later acknowledges.

### 16 — Search & Filters

Module 16 (Search & Filters) is largely DESIGN-only with a thin code stub. Design is well-developed: the full /search facets page (round4-search.jsx, R4Search), the ⌘K palette mockup (portal-states.jsx CmdKOverlay), the FilterBar trigger (portal-components.jsx) and per-list filters all exist. In CODE, only two of nine screens have any reality: (1) a shared CommandPalette (packages/ui) is wired into both AppLayouts behind ⌘K with correct keyboard UX — but it is NAV-ONLY (jumps to screens from the nav config), not entity search, and hits no backend; and (2) per-list search boxes on the portal & workspace OrdersPages that the API serves via plain ILIKE on order title/description (apps/api listOrders.ts). Everything the module is actually about — a global /search page with facets, grouped multi-entity results, PostgreSQL FTS (tsvector+GIN), ts_headline highlight, autocomplete, saved filters/views, and Portal/Blog search — is MISSING from both code and the backend (no /search route, no search_vector column, no SavedFilter model). Docs overstate readiness: DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.9 marks the module ✅ and mislinks it to inbox-screens.jsx (which is Module 18), modules/16-search.md documents an FTS architecture and GET /search that don't exist in code, and DESIGN_SPEC_FULL claims the ⌘K palette has no mockup when CmdKOverlay does exist. The topbar trigger conforms loosely (token-clean) but drops the placeholder copy and ⌘K badge the spec describes.

### 17 — Credentials Vault

Module 17 is a clean case of design-ahead, code-greenfield, docs-stale. DESIGN: design-v2 actually covers the module well across four files — PortalSecrets + Reveal2FAModal + PortalAddSecretModal (portal-secrets.jsx, all 3 portal screens incl. an access journal), C360Secrets + AddSecretModal (workspace-client360-tabs.jsx, typed cards + journal), WorkspaceVault (workspace-content.jsx, the global /vault screen with filters/search/stats), and an older flat ClientCreds (workspace-clients.jsx). CODE: nothing real exists — portal /secrets is the generic Placeholder (App.tsx:50, nav.tsx:28), workspace has no /vault route and ClientDetailPage has no Secrets tab (it's a bare orders list), there is no API route under apps/api/src, and no CredentialVault/CredentialShare model in schema.prisma (only an audit_logs comment mentions credential reveals). So every screen is MISSING/PLACEHOLDER and all conformance is NA. DOCS: DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §5.13.2 (line 549) and modules/17-credentials.md line 12 correctly call the backend greenfield/zero. The drift is concentrated in DESIGN_SPEC_FULL.md §17: its 'Що перевірити' notes #1/#3/#4/#5 and several Мокап-file columns are stale — they say the global vault, the access journal, the Portal secrets screen and the typed 17-Д cards are 'not drawn / nav-only / on no surface', but all of them now exist in design-v2 (the spec text predates the design-v2 delivery that the module doc's line-12 banner already records, and its 'Релевантні файли' still points at the old design/ bundle). The only genuine remaining design gap (already correctly noted) is the reveal flow's countdown/autohide/429 states and a missing 2FA modal on the Workspace side.

### 18 — Chat Hub (Inbox)

Module 18 (Chat Hub / Inbox) is design-complete but code-absent. DESIGN: comprehensive master-detail mockup exists in design-v2/project/inbox-screens.jsx (GlobalInbox portal/workspace, InboxRow, filter tabs, chat-thread + 6 system cards, actions bar, empty states) plus M5 mute/archive/SSE-pulse refinements in workspace-minors.jsx; sidebar nav with unread badge in product-shell.jsx. DESIGN_SPEC_FULL marks the caркас/actions/tabs as РОЗШИР, the row/thread/cards/empty/sidebar as РЕЮЗ, and snooze/responsible/no-reply/search/mute/mobile as НОВЕ. CODE: there is no inbox page — Portal /inbox renders the generic Placeholder (App.tsx:47), and Workspace has no /inbox route and no inbox nav item at all. BACKEND: none of the module's claimed API (GET /messages/conversations, /messages/conversations/:orderId, POST .../read, GET /messages/unread-count, GET /sse/messages) exists; only the underlying module-03 per-order chat (orders/comments.ts + commentsStream.ts SSE) and the OrderChatRead model (schema.prisma:283, used for per-order unread only) are present. ConversationState (mute/archive) is NOT in the schema. DOCS DRIFT: modules/18-chat-hub.md presents the messages API and B/C/D ✅ items as if real (they are spec-acceptance/design-only), uses the stale Portal route name /messages instead of /inbox, and DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §5 ✅ marks are design-only per its own scaffold disclaimer. The portal sidebar item also omits the unread badge the design specifies.

### 19 — Reports

Module 19 (analytics/reports for the agency owner) is almost entirely a design+backend gap. DESIGN side is rich: design-v2 has two reports bundles — workspace-reports.jsx (Overview/Executors/Clients/Departments/Timesheet/Audit nav, marked РЕЮЗ/РОЗШИР) and workspace-reports-v1.jsx (5 v1 reports + XLSX/CSV export + MoM + cash-flow, the 19-А/Б/Д decisions), plus a ReportsHub in workspace-hubs.jsx and empty-states in workspace-missing.jsx. CODE side is nearly empty: there is NO `/reports` route in apps/workspace/src/App.tsx (routes stop at /admin-wallet, catch-all → /), NO "Звіти" sidebar entry, and none of ReportsOverview/Executors/Clients/Departments/Timesheet/Audit exist as pages. The only shipped reporting is owner P&L: backend `/workspace/reports/pnl` + `.csv` (apps/api/src/routes/finance/reports.ts) feeding FinancePage (/finance) and the Owner dashboard. MarginPage (/margin, module 22 turf) covers the by-client margin cut but is not under any reports taxonomy. The Owner dashboard is implemented with finance KPIs but ships NO MoM delta (19-Д) despite the spec claiming the +18% example exists. modules/19-reports.md and DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.13.2 are honest that only P&L exists ("решта звітів — нема aggregation API", 🟡), but DESIGN_SPEC_FULL Part III statuses (РЕЮЗ/РОЗШИР) imply reuse of existing reports screens that have zero code/route, and several Мокап-файл links point to the legacy `design/` path rather than the canonical `design-v2/`.

### 20 — Admin Settings

Module 20 is a DESIGN-COMPLETE, CODE-ABSENT module. Every screen has a design-v2 mockup (workspace-admin-settings.jsx, workspace-admin.jsx, plus reuse of workspace-reports.jsx/portal-states.jsx/product-shell.jsx), and the design files correctly use --wf-_ tokens with the new owner/manager/executor role model. But the frontend has NO admin-settings UI at all: workspace App.tsx exposes zero /admin/_ routes, and the one /settings route renders only payment requisites + referral program (modules 05/09/25), not the admin tabs. Backend is a thin slice: only LegalEntity (20-Д) is real (full admin CRUD API at /workspace/legal-entities, registered, audited, wired into billing/projects, gated by moduleEnabled('legal_entity')), the `manager` role + can() RBAC (20-А) exists in auth, AgencyFeatureFlag/moduleEnabled (Feature-flags) exists, and AuditLog exists but write-only (no read-feed endpoint for the change-journal 20-В). The other 8 settings models (NotificationTemplate, SmtpSender, PdfBranding, ServiceNomenclature, Department, CronRun, WebhookEndpoint, ApiKey) do NOT exist in schema.prisma — exactly as the module doc's own ⚠ legend warns. Net: design ✅ everywhere, code 0% (no screen IMPLEMENTED), backend ranges from real (legal entities, manager-RBAC) to entirely greenfield.

### 21 — System Monitoring

Module 21 (System Monitoring) is design-complete-where-mocked but entirely unbuilt in code. DESIGN: 8 of 19 spec screens have design-v2 mockups — the 5-section dashboard (workspace-monitoring.jsx: health cards, cron heatmap, notif health, audit feed, error log), Notification DLQ ×2 (round4-dlq.jsx), Trash/Purge ×2 (round4-trash.jsx), and 403/Forbidden (round4-misc.jsx); the remaining 11 screens correctly carry '—' (no mockup) and НОВЕ status (cron drill-in, audit detail, full job journal, usage metrics, Telegram alerts, public+admin status-page ×3). CODE: every single screen is MISSING — the spec's anchor route /admin/system (and all sub-tabs DLQ/Trash/Status/Usage/Alerts) does not exist in apps/workspace/src/App.tsx; there is no monitoring/admin/system page directory; gated roles are redirected, not shown the designed 403. No conformance risks to assess since nothing is implemented. BACKEND: partial substrate only — AuditLog and UsageCounter models plus the audit write-service and guarded Sentry wiring exist, and /health+/ready endpoints are live, but all are write-or-internal-only with no UI-facing read/feed/aggregation API; CronRun, StatusIncident, SloTarget, and InfraCost models do not exist at all, blocking the П1 cron heatmap and background-job journal. DRIFT centers on docs overstating readiness: DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §5.1 marks the module '✅ r3' (design-only, reads as built) yet omits it from the §5.13.2 backend-readiness matrix; module-doc body sections still reference cron_runs/cronRun and tag SaaS features ✅ though those models are absent (self-flagged in its own TRACKER caveat).

### 22 — Finance & Expenses

Module 22 (Finance & Expenses) Phase-1 is genuinely built and largely faithful: /finance (Огляд / Витрати / P&L) and /margin (clients/projects) are real React pages backed by working owner-only APIs — expenses CRUD+archive (finance/expenses.ts), P&L (finance/reports.ts), and the margin engine (services/margin.ts, computeProjectMargin/ClientMargin). The backend tenant-guards and owner-gates everything, and the margin '🟢 готово' claim in DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.13.2 is accurate. Drift concentrates in three areas. (1) Biggest gap: the spec marks CSV/XLSX export as РЕЮЗ/П1 'on every report' and the backend pnl.csv endpoint exists, but the implemented FinancePage wires no export button at all — a status-drift where a 'done' cross-cutting feature is functionally missing in code. (2) Conformance: the Overview/P&L LineChart hardcodes two of three series colors (#F87171, #38BDF8) instead of --wf-\* tokens, against the design system and the token-first design source. (3) Several П1 screens the spec anchors to a project detail (per-project margin block at /projects/:id, the zeroCost toggle in EditMemberModal) are unbuilt because no /projects/:id detail route exists — though their backend fields (Project.zeroCost, TeamMember.zeroCostDefault) and computeProjectMargin already exist, so these are wiring/UI gaps, not greenfield. ExpensesTab also ships without the claimed inline filters, and the margin screen lacks the per-executor breakdown. All Phase-2 audit features (receipts, VAT, approval workflow, allocation, budgets, cash-flow, tax report) are correctly labeled НОВЕ and have neither schema models nor UI. One low-severity doc-error: the module doc's prisma Expense block lists fields (amountUsd, notes, linkedExecutorId, source String) that diverge from the real schema, though it carries an explicit 'schema is canon' disclaimer.

### 23 — Leave Tracking

Module 23 (Leave Tracking) is design-only with zero code and zero backend. DESIGN: the four core screens (LeaveExecutor, LeaveAdmin, LeaveRequestModal, LeaveRejectModal) are mockup-complete in design-v2/project/workspace-leave.jsx (+ .css + workspace-leave-data.js), and a settings-side WsLeaves + duplicate LeaveRequestModal live in workspace-calendar-plus.jsx; design files correctly use --wf-_ tokens with hex fallbacks. All НОВЕ extensions (23-А capacity, 23-Б holidays/types settings, 23-В Telegram approval, 23-Г conflict warning, mobile self-service) have no mockups — matching the spec's '—'. CODE: nothing — no /leave or /team/leave route in apps/workspace/src/App.tsx, no page component, the only 'leave' grep hits in apps/workspace are false positives (dragLeave, 'leave the user'). BACKEND: nothing — no /leave route directory in apps/api/src/routes, no LeaveRequest/LeaveBalance/LeaveType in packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma, no leave._ authz, no notification events. So every screen is codeState=MISSING / conformance=NA. DOCS: the module doc (23-leave-tracking.md) honestly says 'Статус: Проєктування', but it points to a non-existent TRACKER.md as status canon, and DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §5.13.2 marks the module '✅ r3' / 'дизайн = 100% готовий' which is true for design but reads as shipped against the rest of the matrix — the main cross-doc inconsistency. Minor route-mapping drift: design source routes admin to /admin/leave while DESIGN_SPEC_FULL uses /team/leave.

### 24 — Calendar

Module 24 (Calendar) is fully designed and entirely unbuilt. DESIGN side: all claimed mockups exist — workspace-calendar.jsx (CalendarMonth/Week/Day, CalEventModal, CalRsvpModal, CalendarEmpty, MobileCalendar, CalLegend), workspace-calendar-plus.jsx (settings/layers/booking-types/leaves), landing-booking.jsx (BookingPage + confirmed), and WSMCalendar in workspace-mobile.jsx. CODE side: zero — no /calendar route or routes/calendar dir in apps/workspace, no Календар sidebar entry, no /book/:userSlug in apps/landing (which only ships / skeleton + /api/health), no /settings/integrations page, no calendar/booking API in apps/api/src/routes, and no CalendarEvent/CalendarAttendee/BookingLink models or CalendarEventType/AttendeeResponse enums in the Prisma schema. So every one of the 21 screens is MISSING / backend=no / conformance=NA. DOCS are largely self-consistent and honest about greenfield status (modules/24-calendar.md:12 and DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.13.2:551 both say 🔴 greenfield, calendar/booking нуль). Real drifts: (1) DESIGN_SPEC_FULL.md:1801 marks the booking-types settings screen Мокап='—' even though CalBookingTypes/BookingTypeModal exist in calendar-plus.jsx; (2) the spec maps screens to /calendar, /book/:userSlug, and /settings/integrations routes that were never scaffolded (mapping-gaps); (3) DESIGN_SYSTEM.md:583 lists Calendar as '(r3) Розблоковано' in the S4 frontend wave, which can read as a code deliverable while the §5.13.2 matrix correctly calls it greenfield.

### 25 — Wallet (Client)

Module 25's core ledger slice is genuinely shipped: Portal /wallet (WalletPage.tsx — bonus balance + money balance + bonus ledger + unified statement) and Workspace /admin-wallet (AdminWalletPage.tsx — companies list, per-company ledger, manual credit/debit adjust modal), all backed by real APIs (/portal/wallet, /portal/wallet/transactions, /portal/wallet/statement, /admin/wallet/companies[/:id/transactions][/adjust], /admin/referral/settings GET+PATCH, /portal/referral). Referral tier settings (РЕЮЗ screen) are implemented too, but live in Workspace /settings (ReferralForm) rather than the Billing→«Реферальні тіри» location the spec claims. The big gaps are every 11.06 owner extension (25-А top-up, 25-Д low-balance, 25-В cashback, 25-Г statement export, S5 bonus-spend, 05-В refund/credit-note/write-off) and the mobile wallet — all design-only or design-mockup-only with no code. Implemented screens use --wf-\* tokens (no hardcoded hex), but several diverge materially from the mockup: Portal hero uses 3 plain Stat boxes instead of two CTA hero cards, drops the filter chips / allocation column / «Правила» tab; Admin omits the StatsRow AR/liability summary + BillingSubtabs and renders the per-company ledger as a Modal rather than the spec's /admin/wallet/companies/:id page route. DESIGN_SPEC_FULL marks 5 of these screens РЕЮЗ/РОЗШИР (done/extend) and links a route that does not exist in code.

### 26 — Leads (CRM)

Module 26 (Leads/CRM) is DESIGN-ONLY: zero code, zero backend. The design-v2 mockup `design-v2/project/workspace-leads.jsx` (439 lines) is rich and covers A1 kanban board (LeadsBoard), A2 lead detail (LeadDetail), A3 convert modal incl. the 26-A "first project in one step" extension (LeadConvertModal), A4 pipeline editor (LeadsPipelines), and B1 the shared WfSource source-icon set — all present and token-based (--wf-\* CSS vars, no hardcoded hex in layout; stage/source brand colors are intentional data values). C1 contact form exists in landing-marketing.jsx but is a plain contact form with NO UTM hidden fields / honeypot / Turnstile, so its "extend" claim overstates the mockup. On the CODE side there is nothing: no `/leads`, `/leads/:id`, or `/settings/leads/pipelines` route in apps/workspace (App.tsx catch-all redirects to `/`), no leads route file, no nav entry (only a comment in nav.tsx:15), no Lead/LeadPipeline/LeadStage/LeadActivity model in prisma schema, and no leads API route (only a stray `'leads'` string in apps/api/src/saas/limits.ts:15 QuotaResource enum). TRACKER.md confirms the whole module is unstarted: LEAD-1/LEAD-2 (and inbound INT-1/INT-3) are all ⬜, GROW-UI is ⏸️ design. DESIGN_SPEC_FULL statuses (РОЗШИР for A1-A4) describe design-intent layered on the design bundle, not code readiness — there is no code base to "extend." The main doc-level drifts: (1) the DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.13.2 "17-module readiness matrix" omits the leads module entirely (no row), and (2) the module's "Релевантні файли" footer in DESIGN_SPEC_FULL points to the old `design-v2/project/workspace-leads.jsx` path while the canonical bundle is `design-v2/`.

### 27 — Integrations Hub

Module 27 (Integrations Hub) is design-complete but entirely unbuilt in code and backend. DESIGN: all screens that have a mockup live in design-v2/project/workspace-integrations.jsx (shell WorkspaceIntegrations + IhKeys/IhKeyModal/IhForm/IhChannels/IhConnectModal/IhWebhooks/IhWebhookModal) plus round4-integrations.jsx (G18); the 6 НОВЕ rows (delivery-detail, webhook-map 27-В, Swagger 27-А, Slack/Discord 27-Б, empty-states, quota-banner) have no mockup, consistent with their '—' Мокап cells. CODE: every screen is MISSING — workspace App.tsx has no /admin/integrations or /settings/integrations route, grep finds zero 'integration' references in apps/workspace/src, the workspace SettingsPage has no integration tabs, and portal /settings/integrations is a Placeholder. BACKEND: none — no ApiKey/WebhookEndpoint/WebhookDelivery/IntegrationConnection Prisma models, no POST /v1/leads, no apikey bearer auth, no webhook fanout/deliver outbox handlers (outbox.ts explicitly defers handlers to S6+); only the durable outbox queue and two quota-resource string literals ('integrations','leads' in saas/limits.ts:15) hint at the seam. DOCS DRIFT: the headline route claim ('/admin/integrations = /settings/integrations, owner-only') is unbacked; the doc's prisma model blocks describe a schema that doesn't exist; and §5.13.2 readiness matrix omits module 27 entirely so its backend state is undocumented. Conformance is NA across the board since nothing is IMPLEMENTED. Net: this is a 🔴 greenfield module presented in spec tables with РЕЮЗ/РОЗШИР design statuses that refer to design-reuse, not code-readiness — a reader could mistake them for build status.

### 28 — Client Management

Module 28 is design-complete but backend-blocked, and the implemented code is a pre-S5 stub far below what the design/status claims imply. DESIGN: the canonical 7-tab Client Card 360° (Огляд·Люди·Проєкти·Фінанси·Документи·Секрети·Активність, with manager Finance-filtering) is fully drawn in design-v2/workspace-client360.jsx + workspace-client360-tabs.jsx, plus modals (reset/deactivate) in the older workspace-clients.jsx, and a cross-client projects list+wizard in workspace-project360-list.jsx. CODE: only three things actually work — the /clients list (orders-derived summary, 4 columns, no real names/tier/LTV/debt/filters/CTA), the /clients/:id detail (NO tabs at all — just stats + an orders table), and a genuinely complete /projects cross-client list with a multi-step create wizard backed by billing/projects.ts CRUD. There is NO clients API at all (no apps/api/src/routes/clients), so every CRM-admin surface — card edit, member management, admin reset-password, deactivate, notes/timeline, finance/secrets/docs tabs — is MISSING. DOCS are mostly honest about the backend blocker (DESIGN_SYSTEM §5.13.2 line 547 and modules/28 line 11 both correctly flag 'НЕМА clients CRUD' 🔴), but DESIGN_SPEC_FULL drifts in three ways: (1) it labels the 360° card 'РОЗШИР' though the code has zero tabs (PLACEHOLDER); (2) its Мокап column for the 360° rows still points at the old 6-tab workspace-clients.jsx instead of the new workspace-client360\*.jsx; and (3) it marks the cross-client /projects list and the create wizard as НОВЕ with 'нема мокапу' even though both design and working backed code now exist.

### 29 — Support (Tickets)

Module 29 is fully greenfield in code, and the docs are honest about it. DESIGN is strong: design-v2 ships two files — workspace-support.jsx (Portal list/new/thread + Workspace queue/thread, shared) and workspace-support-plus.jsx (canned replies, KB list/editor, portal help, CSAT, KB-suggest-on-create) — covering the 6 base screens (РЕЮЗ/РОЗШИР) plus the 7 newer support-plus screens (НОВЕ); all use --wf-\* tokens and correctly implement the internal-note leak-guard (workspace-support.jsx:97). CODE: there is NO backend at all — no Ticket/TicketMessage models in packages/db/prisma/schema.prisma, no /support or /ticket routes under apps/api/src/routes, and no SSE. FRONTEND: Portal /support is a generic Placeholder (apps/portal/src/App.tsx:44), Workspace has NO support route whatsoever (apps/workspace/src/App.tsx has no /support; unknown paths hit catch-all → /), and there is no /support/new, /support/:id, /support/kb, canned-replies, categories, or SLA screen anywhere. So every one of the ~18 screens is MISSING or (for Portal /support only) PLACEHOLDER. The docs match this reality: 29-support.md:11 and DESIGN_SYSTEM.md §5.13 (line 550) both explicitly mark the module 🔴 greenfield / "❌ нуль". The only real drift items are (a) DESIGN_SPEC_FULL maps РЕЮЗ/РОЗШИР screens to mockup files using a stale design/project path in its closing note while the canonical bundle is design-v2/, and (b) DESIGN_SPEC_FULL's per-row "Статус" column (РЕЮЗ/РОЗШИР) describes design-reuse intent and must not be read as code-done — it is purely design status, with zero code behind any row.
